On our controlled benchmark, an MLP binary classifier misclassifies 39.1% of real AI agents as human and a SAINT binary transformer misclassifies 34.5%; adding an explicit agent class yields per-class agent F1 = 1.000 in all 30 runs (3 model families $\times$ 10 seeds). To measure evasion resistance, we construct a five-level evasion ladder spanning passive observation, GAN-generated trajectories, and replay of real human cursor data ($n = 2299$ evasion sessions). Across 10 seeds and 3 model families we observe zero agent misses in 22990 per-seed predictions. The discriminative signal is a browser-automation artifact, not evidence of agent reasoning: Playwright does not emit the raw pointer-move and wheel-delta streams a physical input device produces, and this absence signature survives trajectory manipulation. Exhaustive search over all feature subsets of size 1-5 (9401 GBMs) shows that two behavioral features (mouse_event_rate, teleport_click_ratio) give 100% observed agent recall at every evasion level with agent precision 0.994; five features lift macro-F1 to 0.991. The signal is redundantly encoded: removing teleport_click_ratio leaves agent detection at 100%. The single-feature regime is degenerate, flagging every agent only by collapsing the classifier to always predict "agent". Two features robustly isolate agents; five separate all three traffic classes at macro-F1 $\geq 0.99$.

</details>


### [66] [Belief-Guided Decision Making with Uncertainty Gating in the Game of Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26946)
*Mehrad Yaghoubi,Azam Bastanfard,Abbas Jalilvand,Ashkan Rezaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的信念引导架构，将策略头与独立的信念头解耦，利用记忆机制和门控机制将智能负担从运行时搜索转移到参数化的“直觉”中，显著提升了无搜索胜率并减少幻觉，使在有限硬件上达到专业水平的围棋对弈成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于 AlphaZero 和 MuZero 的计算机围棋进展高度依赖蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）来修正神经网络策略的错误。虽然在大规模计算集群上有效，但这种依赖在消费级硬件上造成关键瓶颈：树管理的计算成本严重限制了推理速率。此外，没有深度搜索时，这些模型会产生幻觉，以高置信度提出策略上致命的着法。

Method: 引入信念引导架构，将策略头与独立的信念头解耦。信念头充当内部模拟器和独立评论家，建模认知不确定性和战略稳定性。集成记忆机制（Transformer/GRU）处理长期依赖和劫规则，并利用门控机制过滤过度自信的策略错误，将智能负担从运行时搜索转移到参数化的“直觉”。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法显著提高了无搜索胜率并减少了幻觉，使在无法进行大规模 MCTS 的有限硬件上实现专业水平的对弈成为可能。

Conclusion: 信念引导架构有效解决了计算瓶颈和幻觉问题，通过将搜索依赖转化为模型内化的直觉，实现了在消费级硬件上的专业级围棋对弈。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Computer Go, driven by AlphaZero and MuZero, rely heavily on Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to correct the errors of the neural network policy. While effective on massive computational clusters, this dependence creates a critical bottleneck on consumer-grade hardware, where the computational cost of tree management severely limits inference rates. Furthermore, without deep search, these models suffer from hallucination, proposing moves with high confidence that are strategically fatal. This paper introduces a novel Belief-Guided architecture that disentangles the Policy head from a distinct Belief head. Unlike traditional value functions, the Belief head acts as an internal simulator and independent critic, modeling epistemic uncertainty and strategic stability. By integrating memory mechanisms (Transformer/GRU) to handle long-term dependencies and the Ko rule, and utilizing a gating mechanism to filter overconfident policy errors, our model shifts the burden of intelligence from runtime search to parametric "intuition." Experimental results demonstrate that this approach significantly improves search-free win rates and reduces hallucination, enabling professional-level play on limited hardware where massive MCTS is infeasible.

</details>


### [67] [Setoka: A Benchmark for Hierarchical User Understanding in Personalized Agents over Heterogeneous Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27056)
*Lingyang Zeng,Guangze Chen,Kaichen Yu,Zhicheng Pan,Siyang Weng,Zirui Hu,Xiangyun Du,Hailin He,Rong Zhang,Chengcheng Yang,Kai Huang,Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Setoka基准，用于评估记忆增强个性化代理的层级用户理解能力，发现现有系统在语义记忆检索表现良好，但在情景记忆、行为模式和人格特质理解上性能显著下降，揭示了跨源整合和长期行为抽象的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准主要评估代理从对话历史中检索显性事实的能力，缺乏对用户抽象个人特征（如行为模式、人格特质）深层理解的评估，无法全面衡量个性化代理的用户理解水平。

Method: 基于认知心理学和人格心理学理论，定义四层用户理解（语义记忆、情景记忆、行为模式、人格特质）；设计基于心理测量学的管道，大规模合成多样、连贯、异构的用户数据与查询，实现真实且隐私保护的评估；结合3个语言模型与5种记忆系统，在10个合成用户上进行综合评测。

Result: 现有系统在语义记忆检索任务上表现优异，但在情景记忆任务上性能下降；在需要整合跨时间分散的异构碎片信息的行为模式和人格特质理解任务上，性能进一步大幅下降。

Conclusion: 用户理解不能仅靠简单事实检索解决，亟需设计支持跨源整合和长期用户行为抽象的记忆机制，以实现更深层的个性化协助。

Abstract: Personalized agents are increasingly applied to assist users across a wide range of tasks. Effective personalized assistance requires not only retrieving explicit facts from past interactions stored in agent memory, but also inferring abstract personal characteristics. However, existing memory benchmarks primarily evaluate whether an agent can retrieve information explicitly stated in conversational histories, failing to provide an effective assessment of deeper user understanding. In this work, we propose Setoka, a benchmark for evaluating memory-augmented personalized agents with hierarchical user understanding from heterogeneous data. Grounded in theories from cognitive and personality psychology, Setoka defines four levels of user understanding, i.e., semantic memory, episodic memory, behavior pattern, and personality trait. Moreover, to enable realistic yet privacy-preserving evaluation, we design a psychometrics-based pipeline that synthesizes diverse, coherent heterogeneous user data and queries at scale. Finally, we leverage Setoka to evaluate 3 language models combined with 5 memory systems for 10 synthetic users. Our comprehensive evaluation reveals that while existing systems perform well on semantic memory retrieval, their performance declines on episodic memory. Moreover, when dealing with behavior pattern and personality trait understanding tasks that require integrating heterogeneous and fragmented information dispersed over time, performance declines even further. These findings demonstrate that user understanding cannot be handled by simple fact retrieval, motivating the design of memory mechanisms for cross-source integration and abstraction over long-term user behavior.

</details>


### [68] [On-Policy Distillation for LLM Safety: A Routing Approach to Template-Robust Realignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27081)
*Yongjian Guo,Wanlun Ma,Lingyu Shen,Xi Xiao,Sheng Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一种名为 ROPD 的新型实时对齐框架，通过建模对齐与被破坏输出概率分布的差异，而非拟合特定提示模板，有效抵御恶意数据投毒攻击，同时保持下游任务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全重对齐防御存在三大局限：导致灾难性遗忘专业技能；在防御者无法观察攻击者提示模板时失效；成功重对齐的模型仍易受简单系统提示切换的再越狱攻击。

Method: 提出 Routing-based On-Policy Distillation (ROPD)，一种建模对齐与被破坏输出概率分布差异而非拟合特定提示模板的新型重对齐框架。

Result: 在三个数据集和三个基础模型上的大量实验表明，基线防御在面临模板不匹配时往往伴随下游任务性能严重下降；ROPD 大幅缓解模板不匹配风险，在防御有效性和能力保持上保持卓越鲁棒性；虽然 ROPD 并非完全免疫模板偏移，但其性能下降可忽略不计，确立了鲁棒 LLM 重对齐的新标准。

Conclusion: ROPD 通过建模输出分布差异而非特定模板，有效解决了现有重对齐方法的三大局限，为抵御恶意数据投毒攻击提供了更鲁棒的解决方案，同时保持了模型的专业能力。

Abstract: Fine-tuning is the dominant paradigm for specializing large language models (LLMs), yet it exposes a critical vulnerability: malicious data providers can embed harmful behaviors into downstream corpora, creating models that retain professional skills while violating human values on demand. Existing safety-realignment defenses often fail in practice due to three key limitations: they frequently cause catastrophic forgetting of specialized skills; their effectiveness collapses when the defender cannot observe the attacker's prompt template; and successfully realigned models remain susceptible to re-jailbreaking via simple system prompt switches. To address these challenges, we propose Routing-based On-Policy Distillation (ROPD), a novel realignment framework that models the divergence between aligned and compromised output probability distributions rather than fitting specific prompt templates. We conduct extensive experiments comparing ROPD against four state-of-the-art baselines across three datasets and three base models with varying alignment strengths. Our results demonstrate that when baseline defenses face template mismatches, often accompanied by severe degradation in downstream task performance. In contrast, ROPD substantially mitigates template-mismatch risks, maintaining superior robustness in both defense effectiveness and capability preservation. While our analysis indicates ROPD is not entirely immune to template shifts, its performance degradation is negligible compared to existing methods, establishing a new standard for robust LLM realignment.

</details>


### [69] [AgentMap: Joint Equivalence and Subsumption Discovery for Ontology Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27130)
*Yiping Song,Jiaoyan Chen,Renate Schmidt,Hui Yang,Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出混合本体匹配（HOM）任务，统一等价与子类发现，并设计基于大语言模型的多智能体框架 AgentMap，在混合、仅等价、仅子类三种设置下均取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统本体匹配仅能发现单一类型语义对应（等价或子类），无法同时发现等价与子类映射，限制了实际应用的灵活性与完整性。

Method: 引入 HOM 任务，设计 AgentMap 框架：针对源本体概念，融合语义检索、层次搜索与多智能体 LLM 协作推理，逐步探索目标本体，识别等价概念或最细粒度父类。

Result: 在扩展的四个 HOM 基准数据集上，AgentMap 在混合设置下表现优异，同时在仅等价与仅子类设置下分别超越对应基线方法。

Conclusion: AgentMap 有效统一了等价与子类发现，为本体匹配提供了更通用、高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Ontology matching (OM) has traditionally been formulated as either equivalence discovery or subsumption matching. The existing OM systems identify only one type of semantic correspondence and cannot simultaneously discover equivalence and subsumption mappings. In this paper, we introduce Hybrid Ontology Matching (HOM), a new OM task that unifies equivalence and subsumption discovery, and accordingly propose a Large Language Model (LLM)-based multi-agent OM framework AgentMap that is implemented by a series of interdependent semantic decisions. Given a concept in the source ontology, AgentMap integrates semantic retrieval, hierarchical search, and collaborative multi-agent LLM reasoning to progressively explore the target ontology, identifying either the equivalent concept, if one exists, or the most fine-grained subsumer. We further extend four OM datasets for a HOM benchmark and evaluate AgentMap under hybrid, equivalence-only, and subsumption-only settings. Experimental results show that AgentMap achieves promising performance on the hybrid setting, and at the same time outperforms equivalence matching and subsumption matching baselines on the equivalence-only and subsumption-only settings, respectively.

</details>


### [70] [Linguistic Monoculture in LLM-Assisted Language Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27134)
*Suhas Thejaswi,Juhi Kulshreshta,Lutz Oettershagen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型辅助写作导致的语言单一化现象，建立数学框架分析三种交互机制下的均衡与多样性演变，指出个体理性导致过度从众的负外部性，个性化模型可保持语言多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型广泛用于写作辅助，可能减少人口层面的语言变异，形成语言单一化，需要理论框架分析不同交互机制对语言多样性的长期影响。

Method: 建立作者和LLM作为语言特征分布的数学框架，分析三种交互机制：固定共享模型、递归更新共享模型、个性化模型；表征均衡与收敛速度；内生化从众为战略选择，构建效用模型权衡清晰度、可读性、感知流畅度与独特风格。

Result: 共享模型驱动作者趋同共同规范；递归反馈重新定位共享规范但不改变成对分布；个性化模型可保持非零语言多样性的作者-模型均衡族。个体理性作者可能过度从众，产生负外部性，单一化代价有限但当独特性主导真实性时可无界增长。合成模拟验证长期多样性结果。

Conclusion: 共享LLM辅助写作会导致语言单一化，个性化模型可缓解该问题；政策层面需考虑个体激励与社会最优的偏差，设计机制保护语言多样性。

Abstract: Writing and communication are increasingly mediated by large language models (LLMs) that are being used to draft, revise and polish text. Although such assistance can improve clarity and help authors meet institutional expectations, widespread reliance on shared models may reduce population-level variation in linguistic form, a phenomenon we refer to as linguistic monoculture. We develop a mathematical framework in which authors and LLMs are represented as distributions over linguistic features and coevolve through repeated interaction. We analyze three interaction mechanisms: a shared model with a fixed linguistic distribution, a shared model recursively updated from author outputs, and personalized models updated through author-specific and population-level feedback. We characterize the resulting equilibria and convergence rates, showing that, shared models can drive authors toward a common norm, recursive feedback relocates the shared norm without altering pairwise spread under common conformity, and personalization can preserve a family of distinct author-model equilibria with nonzero linguistic diversity. We then endogenize conformity as a strategic choice trading off private benefits from clarity, legibility, and perceived fluency against distinctive style. Within this utility model, individually rational authors may conform more than is socially optimal because they do not internalize the value their distinctiveness provides to others, creating a negative externality and a price of monoculture that is finite for each fixed instance but can grow without bound when distinctiveness dominates authenticity. Synthetic simulations illustrate how fixed shared assistance, recursive feedback, and personalization produce different long-run diversity outcomes.

</details>


### [71] [OmegaUse-OfficeVal: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Office-Suite Tasks with Economic Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27155)
*Jingbo Zhou,Yusai Zhao,Qi Bao,Jingjia Cao,Zhenghai Chen,Chang Gao,Kaiqi Guo,Muxin Guo,Mingxuan Li,Xinjiang Lu,Yanru Ma,Yixiong Xiao,Zenghui Zhang,Le Zhang,Hua Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmegaUse-OfficeVal 是一个用于评估大语言模型（LLM）智能体在长时程办公套件任务上表现的基准，包含 100 个源自真实从业者需求的任务，每个任务平均需 2.32 小时人工完成，并提供人工工时与任务价格代理两个经济信号，支持成本-效益对比与价值加权评估。实验显示前沿 LLM 成本和速度远优于人类，但交付质量仍未达人类水平。代码与数据已完全开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏对 LLM 智能体能否以合理成本完成办公套件工作流的评估支持，亟需一个具备任务级经济落地的长时程办公任务基准，以便直接对比人工成本与 LLM 推理成本，并实现价值加权评估。

Method: 从从业者提出的办公套件需求中衍生 100 个任务，经隐私保护流程改编；为每个任务标注人工工时与任务价格代理两个经济信号；基于细粒度评分细则开发代码验证器以保证评估稳定性；在该基准上评测多个前沿 LLM 并设立人类基线。

Result: 所有被测 LLM 的推理成本与耗时均显著低于人工，但交付物质量尚未接近人类水平；任务平均人工耗时 2.32 小时。基准实现了人工成本与 LLM 成本的直接对比及价值加权评估。

Conclusion: OmegaUse-OfficeVal 填补了长时程办公任务经济感知评估的空白，揭示了当前 LLM 智能体在成本优势与质量短板之间的差距，为后续研发提供了可复现、可扩展的开放基准。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly expected to assist users in completing tasks. However, existing benchmarks provide limited support for evaluating whether agents can carry out office-suite workflows at a reasonable cost. We introduce OmegaUse-OfficeVal, a benchmark for evaluating LLM agents on long-horizon office-suite tasks with task-level economic grounding. The benchmark comprises 100 tasks derived from office-suite requests proposed by practitioners and adapted through a privacy-preserving process. On average, these tasks require 2.32 hours of human labor to complete. An important feature of the benchmark is that each task is paired with two economic signals: human labor time and task price proxy. These signals enable direct comparisons between human costs and LLM inference costs, as well as value-weighted evaluation. To support stable evaluation, we develop code-based verifiers from fine-grained rubrics. We evaluate several frontier LLMs together with a human baseline. Although all evaluated LLMs are substantially cheaper and faster than human workers, they have not yet approached human-level deliverable quality. The code and dataset are fully open-sourced, and more information is available on our project website: https://omegause-officeval.github.io.

</details>


### [72] [Partner Capability Estimation for Task-Agnostic Adaptation in Ad-Hoc Teamwork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27177)
*Peter Tisnikar,Maja Swieczkowska,Benteng Ma,Gerard Canal,Matteo Leonetti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CE-CM 及其扩展 CE-CM-Div，通过近似贝叶斯推断任务不变的能力向量，在无需群体预训练的情况下从少量交互中在线估计隐藏伙伴能力，并引入多样化规划器采样以适应人类的次优行为，实现了多任务临时组队中的鲁棒人机协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临时组队（AHT）方法假设任务固定且伙伴能力已知，但现实中伙伴真实能力往往隐藏，且人类协作者可能在多策略任务中表现次优，导致现有方法难以泛化至多任务、动态变化的真实场景。

Method: 1. 将 AHT 重构为隐藏伙伴能力下的联合规划与去中心化执行问题。2. 提出 CE-CM：基于模拟采样的近似贝叶斯方法，推断任务不变的能力向量，构建情境化多智能体马尔可夫决策过程进行规划，无需群体预训练，支持在线少样本信念更新。3. 提出 CE-CM-Div：针对人类行为多样性，用多样化规划器轨迹而非单一最优轨迹评估能力假设，增强对次优行为的鲁棒性。

Result: 仿真实验显示 CE-CM 能快速恢复隐藏能力、减少不可行动作分配并适应能力随时间的变化。离线人类研究（225条轨迹，15名参与者）表明 CE-CM-Div 相比基础 CE-CM 显著提升了能力估计准确性。

Conclusion: 基于能力的建模提供了一种可解释、任务无关的表示，在研究设定中表现出色；考虑行为多样性对于构建鲁棒的人机协作系统至关重要。

Abstract: Effective collaboration with novel and diverse partners is a crucial skill for autonomous agents. Most current ad-hoc teamwork (AHT) approaches assume that agents will collaborate on a single, fixed task and that the partner's capabilities, their ability to successfully execute the desired action, are already known. In reality, a partner's true capabilities are often hidden, and human collaborators may act sub-optimally on tasks with multiple valid strategies. To address these limitations, we extend ad-hoc teamwork into a multi-task setting by re-framing it as a problem of joint planning with decentralised execution under hidden partner capabilities. We introduce CE-CM (Capability Estimation via Contextual Models), an approximate Bayesian method that infers task-invariant capability vectors. By using simulation-based sampling, the agent estimates capabilities and induces a contextual Multi-agent Markov Decision Processes for planning. This approach requires no population pre-training and refines its beliefs online from just a few tasks. To account for human unpredictability, we propose CE-CM-Div, an extension that evaluates capability hypotheses against diverse planner rollouts rather than a single optimal trajectory. Simulated experiments demonstrate that CE-CM rapidly recovers hidden capabilities, reduces infeasible action assignments, and adapts to changes over time. Furthermore, in an offline human study of 225 trajectories from 15 participants, CE-CM-Div substantially improved capability estimates over the baseline CE-CM method. Our results suggest capability-based modelling is a promising interpretable, task-agnostic representation in the studied settings, demonstrating that accounting for behavioural diversity is essential for robust human-AI teaming.

</details>


### [73] [Can AI agents conduct open-ended AI research? Early evidence from two case studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27191)
*Peter Kirgis,Sayash Kapoor,Andrew Schwartz,Stephan Rabanser,David Africa,Konstantinos Voudouris,Viet Nguyen,Toby Pilditch,Magda Dubois,Harry Coppock,Cozmin Ududec,Nitya Nadgir,Matilda Orona,Tilman Bayer,Derrick Chan-Sew,Yue Ling,Abhishek Shetty,Helen Toner,Gillian Hadfield,Seth Lazar,Steve Newman,Shoshannah Tekofsky,Rishi Bommasani,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出「shadow evaluations（影子评估）」新方法评估AI代理进行开放式AI研究的能力：让前沿代理在六天内完成两篇未发表NeurIPS 2026论文的核心研究问题，由原作者评分。结果显示代理虽能独立完成工程实现，但无法在研究问题上取得实质进展，两篇论文均被明确拒稿。识别出五大失败模式：对可发表研究标准判断力差、对研究设计缺陷缺乏创造性应对、从死胡同回溯无效、资源感知差、指令漂移。稳健性检查复现了这些失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估AI代理自动化AI研究能力的方法存在两大缺陷：一是窄任务评估（如可验证编码任务）无法捕捉开放式研究的复杂性；二是将AI生成论文提交盲审，审稿质量参差不齐、随机性大且审稿资源紧张。缺乏一个可靠、可控且能反映真实研究全生命周期的评估范式。

Method: 设计「影子评估」：选取两篇高质量未发表的NeurIPS 2026投稿论文，让前沿AI代理（配备脚手架、数千美元算力、六天时间）自主完成论文的核心开放式研究问题。原论文作者作为专家评审对代理输出打分。同时记录代理完整日志，分析失败模式，并用第二个模型+脚手架组合进行稳健性复现。

Result: 代理能零人工协助完成全部工程实现（代码、实验、绘图等），但在研究问题上均无实质进展，两篇论文被作者明确拒稿。归纳出五类反复出现的失败模式：1) 对可发表研究门槛判断失误；2) 面对设计缺陷缺乏创造性修正；3) 陷入死胡同后回溯无效；4) 算力/时间/预算感知差；5) 长任务中指令漂移。第二模型复现相同失败。

Conclusion: 现有前沿代理已具备AI研究中「工程执行」能力，但仍在「研究判断、创造性问题重构、战略回溯、资源规划、长程一致性」等关键研究生命周期环节显著失效。影子评估提供了一个可复现、专家把关、贴近真实科研流程的新基准，揭示了通往AI研发自动化的核心瓶颈不在编码而在研究决策。

Abstract: Forecasts of explosive AI progress hinge on AI agents automating AI research. But evidence on whether agents can carry out open-ended AI research is thin. Current evaluations either test agents on narrow, verifiable tasks, which excludes open-ended research, or submit AI-generated papers to blind peer review, which is overstretched, stochastic, and suffers from poor review quality. We introduce a third way to measure progress towards AI R\&D automation. An agent takes on the central, open-ended research question of a high-quality unpublished paper, and the paper's original authors grade its output. We call these shadow evaluations. We ran shadow evaluations on two unpublished NeurIPS 2026 submissions, giving frontier agents six days and thousands of dollars of compute. The agents completed all of the engineering without human help, yet could not make substantial progress towards answering the research questions. As a result, both papers were unambiguously rejected by the authors. We identify five recurring failure modes: poor judgment about the bar for publishable research, uncreative responses to shortcomings in the research design, ineffective backtracking from dead ends, poor resource awareness, and instruction drift. A robustness check with a second model and scaffold reproduced these failures. We release the expert reviews, survey responses, agent repositories, and logs. Our results provide early evidence that today's agents can do the engineering of AI research, but struggle with critical parts of the research lifecycle.

</details>


### [74] [Probing the Origins of Reasoning Performance: Representational Quality for Mathematical Problem-Solving in RL vs. SFT Fine-Tuned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26119)
*Antyabha Rahman,Akshaj Gurugubelli,Omar Ankit,Kevin Zhu,Aishwarya Balwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RL训练的大推理模型在数学推理任务上优于SFT模型，其内部表征更线性可分、结构更清晰，且形成层级化架构（深层更关键），而token分配变异性取决于整体训练管线而非单纯RL与SFT的区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究RL训练的大推理模型相对于SFT模型在数学推理任务上优势的机制基础，即内部表征和处理方式的差异。

Method: 1. 在逐层隐藏状态上训练线性探针预测答案正确性，比较RL与SFT模型的线性可分性；2. 进行均值消融研究，分析各层对模型性能的贡献，揭示层级重要性分布；3. 通过重复采样分析token计数变异性，评估自适应计算分配及策略稳定性。

Result: 线性探针显示RL模型预测答案正确性准确率显著高于SFT模型，表明其表征更线性可分、结构更清晰；消融实验显示RL模型呈现层级化架构，深层逐渐变得更关键，而SFT模型各层重要性均匀分布；token计数变异性在RL模型间差异大，部分RL模型变异性高于SFT，部分则高度一致，提示token分配更依赖整体训练管线而非单纯RL与SFT的对比。

Conclusion: RL训练从根本上重构了模型表征和处理推理问题的方式，使其形成层级化、结构化的内部表征；token分配的变异性反映了模型策略的稳定性，揭示了哪些模型表现出稳定策略，哪些处于欠确定、可能非可识别的解行为状态。

Abstract: Large reasoning models trained via reinforcement learning (RL) have been increasingly shown to outperform their supervised fine-tuned (SFT) counterparts on mathematical reasoning tasks; Yet the mechanistic basis for this advantage remains unclear. We therefore ask, what internal representational differences enable RL models' superior performance? Our work presents two converging lines of evidence: First, linear probes trained on layer-wise hidden states reveal that RL models tend to achieve higher accuracy in predicting answer correctness compared to SFT models, indicating more linearly separable and structured representations. Second, mean ablation studies show that RL models develop a hierarchical architecture where deeper layers become progressively more critical, whereas SFT models distribute importance uniformly across layers. Together, these findings demonstrate that RL training fundamentally restructures how models represent and process reasoning problems. Finally, we analyze token-count variability under repeated sampling across problems to assess adaptive compute allocation. While we observe higher variability in some RL-tuned models than in their SFT counterparts, we see strong consistency in others, suggesting that token allocation may depend more on the overall training pipeline than on RL versus SFT alone. We believe this token-allocation variability reveals the spread of plausible on-policy reasoning, highlighting which models exhibit stable policies versus those that are under-determined, potentially non-identifiable solution behaviour.

</details>


### [75] [When benchmark inferences do not compose: Projectibility in AI evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26159)
*Brett Reynolds*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出AI基准测试评估中存在的认识论问题：单个环节的合理链接不能自动构成合理的推论链，提出非组合原则和可投射性审计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI基准测试结果常被泛化、解释、外推、迁移和组合用于下游决策，但以效度为中心的方法要求每个主张都有证据。研究发现即使相邻投射各自合理，它们的组合也可能因端点不匹配、假设不一致、依赖关系和不确定性未传递而失效。

Method: 提出非组合原则：相邻投射的支持仅在端点和假设对齐、依赖和不确定性被传递时才保证其组合合理。通过法律研究案例展示基准证据与部署研究各自合理却平行不交汇；通过再分析和模拟说明聚合稳定性可能抹除后续投射所需的区分；开发可投射性审计方法诊断基准到应用论证中不受支持的连接点。

Result: 案例研究表明：基准证据和部署研究各自合理但保持平行，无法自动组合；模拟显示聚合稳定性会抹除后续投射所需的关键区分；可投射性审计能有效识别基准到使用论证链中未获支持的连接环节。

Conclusion: 相邻投射的支持仅在端点和假设对齐、依赖关系和不确定性被显式传递时，才能保证其组合的合理性。需要系统性的可投射性审计来暴露基准测试到实际部署推论链中的断裂点。

Abstract: An AI benchmark result rarely reaches a consequential claim in one step. Evaluators generalize it to further cases, interpret it as evidence of capability, extrapolate it to new tasks, transport it to another system or site, and combine it with assumptions about human review and downstream consequences. Validity-centred approaches require evidence for each claim. This paper identifies a further epistemic problem: warranted links don't automatically make a warranted chain. The target of one study may not be the source of the next; system, population, outcome, or conditions may change at the interface; and shared data or model lineage may make apparently independent support dependent. Projectibility concerns whether a bounded extension from observed to unobserved cases is warranted. Goodman supplies the problem of rival extensions; argument-based validity supplies an architecture for testing them. The paper's distinctive claim is a non-composition principle: support for adjacent projections warrants their composition only when endpoints and assumptions align and dependence and uncertainty are carried through. A legal-research case shows how benchmark evidence and a deployment study can each be sound while remaining parallel. A reanalysis and simulation show why aggregate stability can erase distinctions a later projection requires. The resulting projectibility audit diagnoses unsupported joins in benchmark-to-use arguments.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [SimpleWikiSearch: A Clean Offline Wikipedia Environment for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26070)
*Guanming Xiong,Penghui Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 SimpleWikiSearch，一个用于可复现评估基于 LLM 的智能体搜索系统的标准化离线环境，明确了语料构建、检索栈、工具契约和评估协议，并在六个 QA 数据集上给出了开源 LLM 的基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体搜索评估往往忽略搜索环境细节（如 Wikipedia 快照、预处理、分块策略、检索后端、工具模式、观察格式、答案提交规则），导致结果难以比较和复现。

Method: 从完整英文 Wikipedia 转储出发，清洗分块，构建关键词和稠密检索索引，暴露最小工具接口（search、open_url、submit_answer），并制定完整评估协议。

Result: 在六个 QA 数据集上报告了多个开源 LLM 的基线性能，并提供随机 300 样本子集以便与闭源商业模型对比。

Conclusion: SimpleWikiSearch 的核心贡献在于提供了一个明确、可运行的参考评测设置，而非新的智能体算法，旨在推动智能体搜索评估的可复现性和可比性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agentic search systems are often evaluated as if the underlying LLM were the only component that matters, yet their measured performance also depends on the surrounding search environment: the Wikipedia snapshot, preprocessing pipeline, chunking policy, retrieval backend, tool schema, observation format, and answer submission rule. These details are frequently under-specified, making it difficult to compare results or reproduce reported baselines. We present SimpleWikiSearch, whose corpus construction, retrieval stack, tool contract, and evaluation protocol are explicit and runnable. The environment starts from a full English Wikipedia dump, cleans and chunks the corpus, builds keyword and dense retrieval indexes, and exposes a minimal tool interface consisting of \texttt{search}, \texttt{open\_url}, and \texttt{submit\_answer}. We report baseline results on six QA datasets using open-source LLMs and provide a random-300 subset for comparisons with closed-source commercial models. SimpleWikiSearch provides a domain-specific agent harness and a controlled offline environment for reproducible agentic-search evaluation. Its contribution is this specified reference setup, rather than a new agent algorithm. Code and data will be available at: https://github.com/JimXiongGM/simple_wiki_search.

</details>


### [77] [GuidedRAG: Semantic Steering of Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26071)
*Matthijs Jansen op de Haar,Tobias Stähle,Lorenzo Gatti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GuidedRAG提出一种新的RAG扩展，引入专用选择阶段和语义引导，在检索前利用语义约束知识库，显著提升检索相关性、精度和用户意图对齐，大幅降低检索开销，并在15种RAG变体上实现全覆盖，验证了其泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进RAG方法依赖日益复杂的检索和知识结构，导致搜索空间庞大、检索精度损失严重、用户意图对齐不足，亟需一种更高效、通用的改进范式。

Method: GuidedRAG引入专用选择阶段和语义引导机制，在检索前利用语义约束知识库，将检索空间与用户意图对齐，从而大幅缩小搜索空间并提升检索质量。

Result: 实验表明：检索相关性提升14.0-15.8%，减轻检索精度损失19.7-27.4%，检索开销降低数量级；相关文本块在排名中更早出现；用户意图对齐提升31.8-36.8%；在15种多样RAG变体上实现全覆盖，展现强泛化性。

Conclusion: 语义引导和选择构成了一个强大且可泛化的范式，能够有效改进当前RAG领域的最先进技术。

Abstract: In this work, we propose GuidedRAG, a novel extension to traditional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) that introduces a dedicated selection stage and semantic steering during retrieval. In contrast to current state-of-the-art RAG approaches, which depend on increasingly complex retrieval and knowledge structures, GuidedRAG constrains the knowledge base using semantics before retrieval, aligning the retrieval space with user intent while substantially reducing the search space. Our evaluation shows that GuidedRAG improves retrieval relevance by 14.0-15.8%, mitigates a 19.7-27.4% loss in retrieval precision, and reduces retrieval overhead by orders of magnitude. Moreover, relevant chunks are consistently retrieved earlier in the ranking process, while alignment with user intent improves by 31.8-36.8%. We further show that GuidedRAG achieves full coverage across 15 diverse RAG variants, demonstrating generalizability across the literature. Together, these findings establish semantic steering and selections as a powerful and generalizable paradigm for improving the current state-of-the-art in RAG.

</details>


### [78] [IFCMemoryBench: Evaluating Long-Term Memory of LLM-Based Agents in BIM Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26072)
*Changyu Du,Alexander Vosseler,Filippo Mazza,André Borrmann*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出IFCMemoryBench基准，评估LLM代理在BIM领域的长期记忆能力，发现当前通用记忆系统在专业工程任务中表现不足，存在领域迁移差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆评估主要关注开放域或人设对话回忆，缺乏在结构化、领域特定的实时环境中复用先前会话信息的测试。BIM专业工程工作流需要代理查询大型IFC模型，同时依赖项目规范、客户决策和工程惯例，这些常在对话中讨论但不在模型中，因此需要更强的测试。

Method: 构建IFCMemoryBench基准，包含143个多会话任务，跨越19个项目和4016个先前会话，源自IFC-Bench v2的不完整信息问题。每个任务在早期对话中植入缺失项目上下文，后续探测问题需结合记忆上下文与实时IFC查询回答。评估框架将记忆性能分解为摄入、检索、利用三阶段，采用专家验证的LLM裁判测量答案质量和记忆质量。评估代表性向量、图、文件基记忆系统。

Result: 最强系统在部署现实摄入范围下仅达32.4%答案准确率，在oracle过滤摄入或更强探测代理下仍低于60%。分析显示通用记忆系统常检索主题相关上下文，但将项目知识存储为不完整或碎片化事实。

Conclusion: 结果揭示代理记忆存在领域迁移差距，可靠的专业代理需要领域感知的记忆表示，将对话、项目知识和结构化模型实体链接。

Abstract: Long-term memory is becoming a core capability of LLM-based agents, but existing evaluations largely test conversational recall in open-domain or persona-grounded settings. We argue that a stronger test is whether an agent can reuse information from prior sessions while acting over a live, structured, domain-specific environment. We study this problem in Building Information Modelling (BIM), a professional engineering workflow where agents must query large IFC models while also relying on project specifications, client decisions, and engineering conventions often discussed in conversation but absent from the model. We introduce IFCMemoryBench, a benchmark for evaluating long-term memory in LLM-based BIM information retrieval. IFCMemoryBench contains 143 multi-session tasks across 19 projects and 4,016 prior sessions, derived from incomplete-information questions in IFC-Bench v2. Each task seeds missing project context across earlier conversations and later asks a probe question that can be answered only by combining remembered context with live IFC queries. Our evaluation framework decomposes memory performance into ingestion, retrieval, and utilization, and measures both answer quality and memory quality with expert-validated LLM judges. We evaluate representative vector-, graph-, and file-based memory systems. The strongest system achieves only 32.4% answer accuracy under a deployment-realistic ingestion scope, and remains below 60% under oracle-filtered ingestion or a stronger probe agent. Analysis shows that current general-purpose memory systems often retrieve topically relevant context but store project knowledge as incomplete or fragmented facts. These results reveal a domain-transfer gap in agent memory and suggest that reliable professional agents require domain-aware memory representations linking conversations, project knowledge, and structured model entities.

</details>


### [79] [IDP AutoOpt: Agent-Driven Optimization of Document Processing Pipeline Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26075)
*David Kaleko,Sergey Ivanov,Md Mofijul Islam*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出IDP AutoOpt，一种自主LLM智能体，可自动发现智能文档处理（IDP）管道的高性能配置，将配置时间从周级缩短至2小时以内，且在提取、分类、数据包拆分等任务上达到或超越人类专家水平，成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前IDP管道的提示词、模型、OCR设置和Schema联合调优需领域专家耗费20-80+人小时/文档类型，且随企业文档类别增加无法扩展，亟需自动化配置方案。

Method: 采用闭环自主智能体：在小规模标注集上评分配置，诊断字段级错误，生成针对性编辑并重新评估，过程由人工编写的领域技能（编码生产经验）指导。

Result: 在医疗、营销情报、金融服务场景的基准测试中，IDP AutoOpt以4.6倍更低的单页成本达到90.2%准确率（人类专家81.6%），配置时间从数周降至2小时内。另发现智能体LLM能力存在硬性阈值，精选领域技能优于原始源码访问，并总结了上下文管理和方差缓解的实践经验。

Conclusion: 仅需可配置管道、评分函数和小规模标注集，该方法即可推广至RAG、多智能体工作流等其他企业级AI系统，解决配置瓶颈，实现大规模部署。

Abstract: We present IDP AutoOpt, an autonomous LLM agent that discovers high-performing configurations for intelligent document processing (IDP) pipelines. Tuning IDP prompts, models, OCR settings, and schemas jointly currently costs domain specialists 20 to 80+ person-hours per document type and does not scale as enterprises add document classes. IDP AutoOpt runs a closed loop: it scores a configuration on a small labeled set, diagnoses field-level errors, generates targeted edits, and re-evaluates, guided by human-authored domain skills that encode production expertise. Across extraction, classification, and packet-splitting tasks deployed in healthcare, marketing-intelligence, and financial-services settings, IDP AutoOpt matches or exceeds human-expert accuracy at equal or lower cost (on an extraction benchmark, 90.2% vs 81.6% at 4.6 x lower per-page cost), cutting configuration time from weeks to under two hours. We further show that agent LLM capability has a hard threshold below which optimization fails, and that curated domain skills outperform raw source-code access, which can degrade performance when provided without structure. We also share practical lessons on context management and variance mitigation. Requiring only a configurable pipeline, a scoring function, and a small labeled set, the approach extends beyond IDP to other enterprise AI systems, such as RAG and multi-agent workflows, where configuration bottlenecks deployment.

</details>


### [80] [FinCacheServe: Dependency-Consistent Answer Reuse for Cost-Efficient RAG Serving over Mutable Enterprise Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26076)
*Lingteng Zeng,Yifan Jin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FinCacheServe 是一种面向可变企业文档的检索增强生成服务缓存系统，通过企业意图索引和多维度依赖守护（文档版本、证据指纹、工具指纹、模型身份、解码配置），实现答案复用，大幅减少 LLM 调用且保证依赖一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业 RAG 服务中存在大量语义等价的分析请求，答案复用可消除 GPU 密集型生成工作，但现有缓存机制难以在文档、证据块、工具输出变更时保证依赖一致性。

Method: FinCacheServe 将每个生成答案视为服务对象，以企业意图为索引，并由文档版本、证据指纹、工具指纹、模型身份和解码配置共同守护；基于 vLLM 实现，在 SEC 金融文档工作负载上用 Qwen2.5 模型评估。

Result: 在 2230 请求的 7B 托管追踪中跳过 53.27% LLM 调用，零依赖过期输出；在三个 32B 算子套件种子（544 请求）中跳过 53.31%，优于版本化语义缓存（38.97%）和 grounded 复用（22.43%）；容量、后端和 SLO 重放显示预言机有界缓存管理、10 万条事务元数据行为，且每依赖新鲜 2s-SLO 成功的估算 Wh 比版本化语义缓存低 44.30%。

Conclusion: FinCacheServe 通过细粒度依赖守护实现了高效且一致的答案复用，显著降低计算成本和能耗，适用于可变企业文档的 RAG 服务。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation services over mutable enterprise documents repeatedly execute semantically equivalent analysis requests. Answer reuse can remove GPU-bound generation work, yet response caches require dependency consistency when filings, evidence chunks, and tool outputs change. FinCacheServe treats each generated answer as a serving object indexed by enterprise intent and guarded by document versions, evidence fingerprints, tool fingerprints, model identity, and decoding configuration. A vLLM implementation evaluates SEC-derived financial-document workloads with Qwen2.5 models. On a 2,230-request hosted 7B trace, FinCacheServe skips 53.27% of LLM calls with zero observed dependency-stale outputs. Across three hosted 32B operator-suite seeds, it skips 53.31% of 544 requests, compared with 38.97% for versioned semantic caching and 22.43% for grounded-style reuse. Capacity, backend, and SLO replays show oracle-bounded cache management, 100k-entry transactional metadata behavior, and 44.30% lower estimated Wh per dependency-fresh 2s-SLO success than versioned semantic caching.

</details>


### [81] [MediaWiki Code2Code Search: Neural Retrieval for the Semantic Discovery of Open-Source Software Entities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26766)
*Francesco Tosoni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 MediaWiki Code2Code Search，一种面向大规模 MediaWiki 生态的语义代码到代码检索系统。通过离线 GPU 索引与在线 CPU 服务分离的架构，结合 FAISS IVF-PQ 压缩索引（168.6 MB，压缩率 96.6%），在 6 GiB 内存限制下实现 1.85 秒中位查询延迟。在 27 个查询基准上，P@10 达 0.87，显著优于 BM25 的 0.64，尤其在名称混淆任务中优势明显。系统已开源并提供 RESTful API。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模代码生态中，用户查询与实现细节存在词汇鸿沟；传统信息检索（IR）延迟低但精度有限，深度学习（DL）精度高但推理慢，难以在资源受限环境（如 Wikimedia Toolforge 的 6 GiB RAM）下部署。需要一种既能捕捉计算意图、又能满足低延迟和内存约束的语义代码检索系统。

Method: 1. 数据构建：从 2,500+ MediaWiki 仓库抽取 129 万结构实体（函数、类型、模板）。2. 分离构建架构：离线阶段利用 GPU 训练神经编码器并生成嵌入；在线阶段仅用 CPU 服务。3. 索引压缩：采用 FAISS IVF-PQ 量化，将 float32 扁平索引压缩至 168.6 MB（压缩率 96.6%）。4. 检索流程：查询编码 → IVF 粗选 → PQ 精排 → 返回 Top-k 结果。

Result: 在 27 查询基准上：P@10 0.87（BM25 0.64），严格匹配下 0.52 对 0.34。中位查询延迟 1.85 秒，满足 6 GiB RAM 约束。名称混淆任务中性能提升最显著，词法方法失效而语义检索依然有效。

Conclusion: MediaWiki Code2Code Search 证明了在资源受限环境下部署大规模语义代码检索的可行性。分离构建架构与 FAISS IVF-PQ 压缩有效平衡了精度、延迟与内存占用。系统已开源（Apache 2.0）并提供公开 API，为代码复用、迁移与理解提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Code search in large-scale ecosystems is often hindered by the lexical gap between user queries and implementation details, alongside the trade-off between the low latency of traditional Information Retrieval (IR) and the precision of Deep Learning (DL). We present MediaWiki Code2Code Search, a neural retrieval system for semantic code-to-code discovery. By indexing 1.29 million structural entities (functions, types, and templates) across 2,500+ MediaWiki repositories, our system enables retrieval based on computational intent rather than surface tokens. We employ a split-build architecture, decoupling GPU-intensive offline indexing from a CPU-only serving layer; our FAISS IVF-PQ index occupies 168.6 MB: a 96.6\% reduction compared to a flat float32 baseline, and achieves a median query latency of 1.85 seconds on commodity hardware, satisfying the 6 GiB RAM constraint of Wikimedia Toolforge. Our evaluation across a 27-query benchmark demonstrates superior performance over the BM25 baseline, achieving a P@10 of 0.87 compared to 0.64 (0.52 versus 0.34 for strict matching). Gains are most pronounced in name-obfuscated tasks where lexical methods fail. The system is available at https://code2codesearch.toolforge.org under the Apache 2.0 licence and provides an open RESTful API.

</details>


### [82] [WhisperRec: Latent Reasoning for Efficient Foundation Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26621)
*Hao Jiang,Peiru Du,Pengfei Yao,Mengting Li,Siyuan Lou,Kuo Cai,Sheng Yu,Qiang Luo,Jian Liang,Ruiming Tang,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang,Wenwu Ou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: WhisperRec提出了一种高效的潜在推理框架，通过将教师生成的显式CoT压缩为可学习的潜在推理token，实现了潜在空间中的推理，避免了冗长的自回归生成开销，同时保留决策相关的推理信息。引入多视角自适应CoT（MV-ACoT）构建多样化高质量监督，并通过三阶段潜在推理对齐和课程式后训练激活潜在推理能力。实验表明其在推荐性能和推理吞吐率上显著优于显式CoT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的基础推荐模型（FRM）通常采用显式Chain-of-Thought（CoT）的Think-then-Answer范式，但生成冗长理由带来巨大推理开销，且固定CoT模板难以建模多样化、动态、上下文依赖的用户兴趣。

Method: 提出WhisperRec框架：1) 多视角自适应CoT（MV-ACoT）从互补视角构建多样化、高质量的教师CoT监督，并根据实例难度自适应调整推理复杂度；2) 三阶段潜在推理对齐过程，将教师CoT逐步内化为潜在表示；3) 基于课程的后训练激活潜在token推理能力，同时保留标准推荐能力。

Result: 在工业级快手数据集和公开Kuaishou LLM-Rec基准上，WhisperRec持续优于显式CoT方法和常规基线。相比显式CoT Think和No-Think变体，SID@64分别提升17.44%和9.33%，在线推理吞吐率提升10倍以上。

Conclusion: WhisperRec通过潜在推理范式有效解决了显式CoT的推理延迟和建模局限问题，实现了高效且高性能的基础推荐模型推理，为大规模工业推荐系统提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning capabilities, motivating their adoption as backbones for foundation recommendation models (FRMs). Existing approaches typically enhance recommendation with explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) under the Think-then-Answer paradigm. However, generating lengthy rationales introduces substantial inference overhead, while fixed CoT templates struggle to model diverse, dynamic, and context-dependent user interests. We propose WhisperRec, an efficient latent reasoning framework for FRMs. WhisperRec compresses teacher-generated CoT into learnable latent reasoning tokens, enabling a Latent-Reason-then-Answer paradigm that performs reasoning in latent space without producing verbose rationales. This design retains decision-relevant reasoning information while avoiding the latency bottleneck of autoregressive rationale generation. Specifically, it first introduces Multi-View Adaptive CoT (MV-ACoT) to construct diverse, high-quality supervision from complementary perspectives on user interests. MV-ACoT also adapts reasoning complexity to each instance, applying lightweight analysis to clear cases and targeted multi-factor reasoning to challenging ones. Building on a pre-trained FRM, WhisperRec then employs a three-stage Latent Reasoning Alignment procedure to progressively internalize teacher CoT into latent representations. Finally, curriculum-based post-training activates latent-token reasoning for downstream recommendation while preserving standard recommendation capability. Experiments on an industrial-scale Kuaishou dataset and the public Kuaishou LLM-Rec benchmark show that WhisperRec consistently outperforms explicit-CoT methods and conventional baselines. Compared with explicit CoT Think and No-Think variants, WhisperRec improves SID@64 by 17.44% and 9.33%, respectively, and achieves over 10x higher online inference throughput.

</details>


### [83] [Improving Item Discoverability in e-Commerce Search via Related Intent Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27172)
*Ji Xin,Xiao Xiao,Ishan Bhatt,Vinesh Gudla,Trace Levinson,Raochuan Fan,Shishir Kumar Prasad,Prakash Putta,Tejaswi Tenneti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种可扩展的发现增强搜索系统，通过意图条件召回扩展提升电商搜索中的商品发现率。采用两阶段混合架构：大语言模型处理头部查询，微调小语言模型（LoRA+蒸馏）处理尾部查询。实验显示发现覆盖率从60%提升至80%，推理成本仅为教师模型的30%，且有助于长尾商品曝光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统搜索系统优先精准匹配，牺牲召回率，在电商和生鲜场景下限制了替代品、互补品及主题相关商品的可发现性，影响用户满意度和商业成果。

Method: 两阶段混合架构：第一阶段使用闭源大语言模型为头部查询生成隐式意图以最大化可发现性；第二阶段通过LoRA适配器和教师-学生蒸馏微调小语言模型，将该能力扩展至尾部查询，实现意图条件召回扩展。

Result: 系统显著提升意图生成质量和下游检索效果，发现覆盖率从约60%提升至80%的查询流量，推理成本仅为教师模型的约30%。双重评估框架（LLM-as-a-judge指标经人类偏好验证，以及端到端会话级购买分析）均确认了改进效果。

Conclusion: 发现增强搜索不仅提升相关性，还可作为市场平衡机制，为长尾和新兴供给提供查询条件下的曝光机会，为大规模市场部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Traditional search systems are optimized to retrieve items that strictly match a query, often prioritizing precision over recall. In e-commerce marketplaces and particularly grocery, this paradigm is limiting, as user satisfaction and commercial outcomes depend heavily on the discoverability of substitute, complementary, and thematically related items. In this paper, we present a scalable system for discovery-augmented search that leverages intent-conditioned recall expansion. Our approach generates implicit user intents to expand candidate recall while maintaining relevance.
  The system addresses the cost-quality tradeoff of generative retrieval through a two-stage hybrid architecture. First, we leverage closed-weight large language models (LLMs) to maximize discoverability for head queries. To extend these benefits to tail queries, we then introduce a finetuned small language model (SLM), trained via LoRA adapters and teacher-student distillation. We evaluate the system using a rigorous dual framework: (a) LLM-as-a-judge metrics validated against human preferences for semantic quality, and (b) end-to-end session-level purchase analysis. Results demonstrate that our approach improves both intent generation quality and downstream retrieval effectiveness, extending discovery coverage from approximately 60% to 80% of query traffic at roughly 30% of the teacher model's inference cost, offering a viable path for deployment in large-scale marketplaces. Beyond relevance gains, discovery-augmented search may serve as a marketplace-balancing mechanism, giving long-tail and emerging supply an opportunity for query-conditioned exposure.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [Shared SFT Lessons Across Alignment, Model Organisms, and Toy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26173)
*Anton de la Fuente,Arthur Conmy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种在不同监督微调（SFT）设置间迁移经验的案例：1）将对齐训练中关于行为泛化的经验（训练行为原因而非仅示例）迁移到玩具模型；2）将模型生物学中关于能力保持的经验（离模型输出损害能力，混合良性在模型数据可缓解）迁移到Model-Spec Midtraining对齐设置；3）将模型生物学中关于鲁棒性的经验（后续良性SFT可抹除对齐行为但保留能力）迁移到同一对齐设置。结果表明跨领域迁移SFT经验能提升各研究领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐训练、模型生物学和玩具模型通常被视为独立研究领域，但它们常使用SFT追求相同底层目标。当项目共享目标时，应测试一个领域的经验是否能迁移到其他领域。

Method: 研究三种跨领域经验迁移：第一，将对齐训练中'Teaching Claude Why'的方法（训练行为原因）迁移到玩具模型，测试行为泛化；第二，将模型生物学中发现的离模型输出损害能力的现象迁移到Model-Spec Midtraining，测试混合良性在模型/在策略数据是否能缓解损害并嵌入目标行为；第三，将模型生物学中的鲁棒性经验迁移到对齐设置，测试后续良性SFT是否能抹除对齐行为同时保留能力。

Result: 1）训练行为原因比仅训练行为示例能更好地实现行为泛化；2）混合良性在模型/在策略数据能防止大部分能力损害，同时仍能嵌入目标行为；3）后续良性SFT可抹除对齐行为但保留能力，表明能力保持不足以保证对后续训练的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 跨领域迁移SFT经验可以提升所有相关研究领域，建议更多研究者从自身领域外借鉴技术。

Abstract: Alignment training, model organisms, and toy models are usually treated as separate research areas. But projects in all three frequently use supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to pursue the same underlying goals. When projects share a goal, we should test whether lessons learned from one area transfer to the other areas. We study three such transfers, each taking a lesson developed in one SFT setting and testing it in another. First, we port a lesson about behavior generalization from alignment training into toy models. Training on the reason for a behavior, as in Teaching Claude Why, can make the behavior generalize better than training on examples of the behavior alone. Second, we port a lesson about capability preservation from model organisms into the Model-Spec Midtraining alignment setting. SFT on outputs written by a model other than the student (off-model outputs) can damage capabilities when trained on. Mixing in benign on-model (and on-policy) data into our training can prevent most of this damage while still embedding the target behavior. Third, we port a lesson about robustness from model organisms into the same alignment setting. We find that follow-up benign SFT can erase the alignment behavior while preserving capabilities, showing that capability preservation alone does not ensure robustness to subsequent training. Our work illustrates how porting SFT lessons between different research fields can uplift them all, suggesting more researchers should borrow techniques from outside their own areas.

</details>


### [85] [Weak-to-Strong On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26246)
*Fangxu Yu,Zinan Lin,Xiaodong Liu,Weijia Xu,Michael Xu,Tianyi Zhou,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了弱到强的在线策略蒸馏框架，通过对比弱模型对的logit差异构建代理教师，使强学生模型能够从多个更弱的模型中蒸馏知识，在数学和代码基准上超越现有OPD方法和领域教师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线策略蒸馏方法假设教师模型至少与学生同等强：要么蒸馏更大模型到更小模型（在前沿领域无更大教师可用），要么整合从共享基座训练的多个领域专家（需要在学生规模上进行昂贵训练）。需要一种方法让强学生模型能够从更弱的监督源中获益。

Method: W2S-OPD在logit空间从一对正负对比模型（均小于学生且易于获取）构建代理教师。两者的logit差异隔离了能力方向，加到学生的基座模型上，得到一个耦合该方向且分布上贴近学生的代理教师。学生通过在自身rollouts上最小化逐token反向KL来蒸馏该代理教师。实例化三种对比对：(i) 后RL专家 vs 预RL初始化，隔离RL注入的技能；(ii) 更大 vs 更小基座模型，隔离规模带来的能力；(iii) 带正确提示 vs 错误提示的小基座模型，隔离实例级的解题方向。

Result: 在4个数学和3个代码基准上，W2S-OPD优于OPD，使学生超越领域教师，且即使所有监督源都更弱时仍能持续提升学生。分析显示不同对比产生不同信号：后RL和提示对比强调推理框架，规模对比强调解题流程。

Conclusion: W2S-OPD是一个简单有效的弱到强蒸馏框架，打破了教师必须强于学生的假设，实现了从多个弱模型向强模型的能力迁移，为前沿模型的自我改进提供了新范式。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD), which aligns a student with the teacher's token-level distribution on the student's own rollouts, is an effective paradigm for transferring capabilities across LLMs. Prevailing approaches assume a teacher at least as capable as the student: they either distill a larger model into a smaller one, which fails at the frontier where no larger teacher exists, or consolidate multiple domain experts trained from a shared base, which requires costly training at the student's scale. We introduce Weak-to-Strong On-Policy Distillation (W2S-OPD), a simple yet effective OPD framework that improves the strong student by distilling from multiple weak models. W2S-OPD constructs a proxy teacher in logit space from a contrast pair of a positive and a negative model, both smaller than the student and cheap to obtain. Their logit difference isolates the capability direction, which is added to the student's own base model, yielding a proxy teacher that couples this direction while staying distributionally adjacent to the student. The student then distills it by minimizing the per-token reverse KL on its own rollouts. We instantiate the contrast pair as i) a post-RL expert against its pre-RL initialization, isolating the skill RL instills, ii) a larger against a smaller base model, isolating the capability from scale, and iii) a small base model with correct versus wrong hints, isolating the instance-level direction toward the solution. Across four math and three code benchmarks, W2S-OPD outperforms OPD, enables the student to surpass the domain teacher, and keeps improving the student even when every supervision source is weaker. Analysis shows different contrasts yield distinct signals: the post-RL and hint contrasts emphasize reasoning frameworks, while the scale contrast emphasizes the solving procedure. Our code will be available at https://github.com/Yu-Fangxu/W2S-OPD.

</details>


### [86] [Between Gradient and Natural Gradient: A Continuum of LoRA Initializations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26247)
*Dianze Liu,Farshid Ghezelbash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出统一LoRA(ULoRA)框架，将梯度投影与曲率白化两类初始化方法统一为由谱白化指数和Adam式对角指数构成的双参数连续族，证明最优预条件强度依赖任务且常位于家族内部，并给出无需搜索的ULoRA-Auto变体，利用层级谱统计自动选择指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA初始化方案（如梯度投影到主方向、或用损失曲率估计白化）看似截然不同，实则是同一设计空间的两个端点；固定预条件强度无法在所有下游任务上取得最优，需将初始化与曲率预条件视为可调维度而非固定设计决策。

Method: 构建两参数族ULoRA，引入谱白化指数控制曲率白化程度和对角指数控制Adam式自适应缩放；在完整学习率搜索下扫描该家族；提出可部署变体ULoRA-Auto，根据各层实测谱统计自动设定逐层指数，无需额外超参数搜索。

Result: 调优后的ULoRA在RoBERTa-base上五个GLUE任务均匹配或超越全参数微调；在LLaMA-2-7B上GSM8K任务与最强基线竞争；ULoRA-Auto零搜索成本逼近上界，在可部署LoRA方法中排名靠前。

Conclusion: LoRA初始化与曲率预条件的原则性设计空间应作为可调超参数维度处理；ULoRA提供统一理论框架与自动化实现，消除了在固定端点间二选一的必要。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) fine-tunes large pretrained models at a fraction of the cost of full fine-tuning, but its performance depends strongly on how the adapters are initialized. Recent schemes initialize the adapters from the downstream loss gradient: some project the raw gradient onto its top directions, while others first whiten it with an estimate of the loss curvature. We show that these seemingly distinct methods are points on a single continuum: a two-parameter family of preconditioned gradient initializations, which we call Unified LoRA (ULoRA), governed by a spectral whitening exponent and an Adam-like diagonal exponent. Sweeping this family under a full learning-rate search, we find that no single fixed preconditioning strength dominates: the best operating point is task-dependent and frequently lies strictly inside the family, away from the published endpoints. Treated as an upper bound of this family, a tuned ULoRA configuration matches or exceeds full fine-tuning on all five GLUE tasks with RoBERTa-base and is competitive with the strongest baselines on GSM8K with LLaMA-2-7B. Our deployable, search-free variant, ULoRA-Auto, selects per-layer exponents from measured spectral statistics, approaches this upper bound at no additional search cost, and ranks at or near the top among deployable LoRA methods. Our results show that a principled design space for LoRA initialization and curvature preconditioning should be treated as a tunable dimension rather than a fixed design decision.

</details>


### [87] [Early Verdicts, Better Budgets: Sequential Adaptive Rollout Allocation for Compute-Efficient RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26253)
*Pixel Nomand,Elena Voss,Marcus Hale,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SARA（顺序自适应Rollout分配），将RLVR中的每步Rollout收集建模为预算约束的序列分配（最优停止）问题。SARA维护各提示词成功率的Beta后验，利用闭式预测器评估组有效性，采用双阈值SPRT式规则：提交有效组、在短探测后放弃饱和组、将释放预算重分配给新提示词，无需额外预测Rollout。理论证明了放弃可靠性、预期Rollout节省、固定预算产出优势，并建立了有效组产出与GRPO梯度范数的联系。实验显示，SARA在1.5B/3B模型上匹配DPS（均低于DS Oracle），Rollout减少22%；与DPS组合后准确率最高，略超DS，Rollout减少67%（接近均匀成本）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR受Rollout生成瓶颈制约，大量采样提示词产生饱和组（全对/全错），奖励方差为零，无策略梯度信号。现有补救要么动态采样（过采样再丢弃）付出大量额外Rollout，要么预测提示词难度，但策略漂移下脆弱。观察到组有效性通常在前几个Rollout即可决定，继续在已定提示词上消耗完整组预算是浪费。

Method: 将每步Rollout收集建模为预算约束的序列最优停止问题。SARA为每个提示词维护成功率的Beta后验，推导闭式组有效性预测器，应用双阈值SPRT式规则：当预测器超过上阈值则提交该组，低于下阈值则在短探测后放弃，释放的预算即时重分配给新提示词。全程无需额外预测Rollout。

Result: 理论：证明放弃可靠性、预期Rollout节省、固定预算产出占优，并连接有效组产出与GRPO梯度范数。实验：数学推理与规划任务，1.5B/3B模型单GPU。SARA单独使用匹配DPS（均低于DS Oracle），Rollout减少22%；SARA+DPS组合达最高准确率，略超DS，Rollout减少67%（近均匀成本）。

Conclusion: SARA通过序列自适应分配从根本上解决RLVR中饱和组浪费Rollout的问题，无需额外预测开销，理论保证与实验均展示显著样本效率提升，且可与现有动态采样正交组合进一步增强。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is bottlenecked by rollout generation, yet many sampled prompts produce saturated groups (all responses correct or all incorrect) whose zero reward variance yields no policy-gradient signal. Existing remedies either oversample a larger candidate pool and discard saturated prompts (dynamic sampling), paying heavy extra rollouts, or predict prompt difficulty before sampling, which is fragile under a shifting policy. We observe that a group's effectiveness is usually decided early, within the first few of its rollouts, so spending a full group on an already-decided prompt is wasteful. We cast per-step rollout collection as a budget-constrained sequential allocation (optimal stopping) problem and introduce SARA (Sequential Adaptive Rollout Allocation). SARA maintains a Beta posterior over each prompt's success rate, evaluates a closed-form predictor of group effectiveness, and applies a two-threshold, SPRT-style rule that commits effective groups, abandons saturated ones after a short probe, and reallocates the freed budget to fresh prompts, without any extra prediction rollouts. We prove abandonment reliability, expected rollout savings, fixed-budget yield dominance, and a link between effective-group yield and the GRPO gradient norm. On mathematical reasoning and planning with 1.5B/3B models on a single GPU, SARA matches DPS (both below the DS oracle) while using 22% fewer rollouts than DS; composing SARA with DPS yields the best accuracy, slightly above DS, at 67% fewer rollouts (near-uniform cost).

</details>


### [88] [Top-$k$ Pareto Bandits: Hypervolume Regret for Multi-Objective Slate Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26273)
*Nicolas Gutowski,Fabien Chhel,Alexandre Letard,Sylvain Lamprier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究随机多目标组合老虎机问题：每轮选择k个臂的组合，观察d维奖励向量（半反馈）。目标是维护一个小规模动作集合共同近似帕累托前沿，用被支配超体积形式化目标，定义α近似超体积遗憾（α=1-1/e）。提出THV-UCB算法，基于乐观边际超体积贡献贪心选择臂。证明了无间隙遗憾界Õ(d√(nkT))和依赖间隙界Õ(nk^{2.5}/Δ_min)，后者在臂充分分离时呈多项式对数级。为多目标应用中用小子集近似帕累托前沿提供理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多目标老虎机工作多聚焦于识别单个最优臂或整个帕累托前沿，但实际应用中往往只需维护一个小规模、多样化的动作集合来近似前沿。论文旨在形式化这一目标：利用超体积指标刻画子集对帕累托前沿的覆盖质量，并设计高效算法在半反馈下学习近似最优的k臂子集，解决多目标决策中的探索-利用权衡。

Method: 提出THV-UCB算法：维护每个臂奖励向量的置信上界，计算其对当前已选集合的边际超体积贡献的乐观估计，贪心选择边际贡献最大的臂构建大小为k的组合。利用超体积函数的单调次模性，贪心策略保证(1-1/e)近似比。算法在半反馈下仅需观测所选臂的奖励，计算高效。

Result: 理论保证：1) 无间隙遗憾界Õ(d√(nkT))，对所有实例均成立；2) 依赖间隙遗憾界Õ(nk^{2.5}/Δ_min)，当最小间隙Δ_min足大时退化为多项式对数级。两者均匹配问题维度依赖，验证了THV-UCB在近似帕累托前沿任务上的最优性。

Conclusion: 论文为随机多目标组合老虎机中的帕累托前沿近似问题提供了首个理论框架，通过超体积遗憾形式化目标，设计了具有近似保证的乐观算法THV-UCB，并建立了紧致的遗憾界。结果表明，在半反馈下维护小规模动作集合近似多目标前沿是可行且高效的，为多目标推荐、资源分配等实际应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider a stochastic multi-objective bandit problem where, at each round, the agent selects a slate of $k$ arms and observes their $d$-dimensional reward vectors under semi-bandit feedback. We do not aim at identifying a single optimal arm; instead, we consider the problem of maintaining a small set of actions that jointly approximate the Pareto frontier. We formalize this objective through the dominated hypervolume induced by the selected subset of arms, and define an $α$-approximate hypervolume regret with respect to the best size-$k$ subset achievable in hindsight, where $α= 1 - 1/e$ reflects the approximation guarantee of greedy maximization for monotone submodular functions. To address this problem, we introduce \textit{THV-UCB}, an optimistic algorithm that selects arms greedily based on optimistic estimates of their marginal hypervolume contributions. We establish a gap-free regret bound $\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{nkT})$ that holds on every instance, together with a gap-dependent bound $\tilde{O}(nk^{2.5}/Δ_{\min})$ that becomes polylogarithmic in $T$ once the arms are sufficiently well separated. Our results provide theoretical support for using small subsets to approximate Pareto fronts in various multi-objective applications.

</details>


### [89] [FloDR: An invertible dimensionality reduction method based on a normalising flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26278)
*Abdallah Baraka,Daniel Probst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FloDR 是一种基于可逆归一化流的降维方法，在生成二维嵌入的同时保留高维信息，并提供条件扩散与隐藏对比两个诊断可视化场，配合统计检验与 bootstrap 置信度，解决传统方法（如 t-SNE、UMAP）因优化丢弃信息导致聚类距离、空白区域含义及隐藏结构不可见的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二维降维方法（t-SNE、UMAP 等）在优化过程中丢弃高维信息，导致嵌入图中聚类间距离、空白区域语义及每点隐藏结构均无法直接读取，缺乏可靠的诊断手段。

Method: 采用可逆归一化流将高维数据映射到等维空间，仅取前两维作为二维嵌入，其余维度保留不丢弃。利用流模型的精确逆映射与精确密度估计，计算两个诊断场：条件扩散（conditional spread，衡量每个嵌入位置在输入空间中仍未确定的数据量）与隐藏对比（hidden contrast，衡量二维坐标丢弃的标签对比信息）。两场均在预留测试集上进行预设定统计检验，并给出 bootstrap 置信区间；未通过检验的场标记为“拒绝”不予显示。

Result: 成功构建了可解释的二维嵌入及配套诊断可视化工具，条件扩散场揭示局部不确定性，隐藏对比场量化标签信息损失，统计检验与拒绝机制保证了可视化结果的可靠性。

Conclusion: FloDR 通过可逆流保留完整高维信息，提供了带统计保证的诊断可视化，克服了传统降维方法信息丢失、不可解释的根本局限，为高维数据探索提供了更严谨的框架。

Abstract: It is common for two-dimensional embeddings of high-dimensional data to be read far beyond what they can support. Distances in and between clusters, the meaning behind empty spaces, and the amount of structure hidden at each point are generally invisible in the output of methods such as t-SNE and UMAP. This is because the information that could support the meaning of these properties is discarded during the optimisation process. Here, we present FloDR, a dimensionality reduction method that embeds data through an invertible normalising flow. While FloDR only uses the first two output coordinates to create a two-dimensional embedding, it retains the remaining coordinates rather than discarding them. In addition to the embedding, an exact inverse and an exact density are properties of a trained mapping, which enable diagnostic visualisations that are computed from the exact inverse of the model that drew the layout rather than from an approximate one. Specifically, we draw two fields, the conditional spread, which measures how much of the original data remains undetermined at each embedding position in input units, and the hidden contrast, which measures how much information about a labelled contrast the two plotted coordinates discard. Both fields are rendered with a prespecified test against a held out portion of the input data and a bootstrap confidence. A field that fails the test is reported as refused.

</details>


### [90] [Entity Resolution in Practice: Lessons from a Self-Serve Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26298)
*Kaushik Pavani,Ganga Aluri,Pravin Jadhav,Neeraj Prasad,Kiran Sanka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 总结：自助式实体分辨系统在六个基准测试上揭示三个关键经验：无单一算法通用、精确率与召回率需分开优化、需主动重验跨组合并以防错误链式传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：现有实体分辨文献缺乏对自助式系统在多样化数据集上表现的系统性研究，导致从业者经历大量无效实验。

Method: 方法：构建并评估自助式实体分辨系统，在包含864至500万条记录的六个基准上测试，对比多种算法家族，分析精确率/召回率优化策略及传递性合并的风险。

Result: 结果：发现三条经验：(1) 需训练多算法家族并自动选优；(2) 精确率靠硬规则否决，召回率靠多样化候选检索；(3) 单一假阳性链接可通过传递性错误合并数百记录，必须主动重验跨组合并。

Conclusion: 结论：这些经验可帮助从业者避免数月无效实验，推动自助式实体分辨系统的实用化设计。

Abstract: We built and evaluated a self-serve entity resolution (ER) system on six benchmarks spanning 864 to 5M records, and three lessons emerged that are absent from existing ER literature. (1) No single matching algorithm wins everywhere - a self-serve pipeline cannot predict its next dataset, so we recommend training several algorithm families per dataset and letting an automatic bake-off pick the winner. (2) Precision and recall need separate fixes, not a shared threshold - precision needs hard rule-based vetoes, recall needs more diverse candidate retrieval. (3) One false-positive link can silently merge unrelated entities - assuming "A matches B" and "B matches C" implies "A matches C" lets a single bad link chain hundreds of records together, so every cross-group merge must be actively re-verified. We hope these lessons save practitioners the months of dead-end experiments that led us to them.

</details>


### [91] [Learning Implicit Causal World Models from Multi-Agent Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26336)
*Jasorsi Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出隐式因果世界模型，从离线演示中恢复环境动力学，无需预定义因果图，通过策略方差使世界模型满足序列后门条件，在多智能体协作任务中实现可解释的因果表示，且模型精度随干预强度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的强化学习中，世界模型训练常混淆统计相关性与因果机制，尤其在多智能体系统中，物理转移与策略意图交织，导致模型在分布偏移下失效。

Method: 引入隐式因果世界模型，利用策略方差使世界模型可通过序列后门条件被发现，从而在无需预定义因果图的情况下从离线演示中恢复环境动力学。

Result: 在Two-Door、Navigation、Giveway等协作任务中评估，模型在完全可观测和部分可观测条件下均提供可解释的因果表示，模型准确度随干预强度直接提升。

Conclusion: 隐式因果世界模型能有效解决多智能体系统中世界模型因混淆相关性与因果性导致的分布偏移问题，实现了无需预定义因果图的因果动力学恢复，且具有良好的可解释性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: In model-based reinforcement learning, world models exist as internal simulators, but their training often conflates statistical correlations with causal mechanisms. This problem is exacerbated in multi-agent systems where physical transitions are intertwined with strategic agent intents, causing world models to fail under distribution shift. We introduce Implicit Causal World Models to recover environmental dynamics from offline demonstrations without requiring pre-defined causal graphs. By incorporating policy variance, we render world models discoverable via the sequential backdoor condition. Evaluations across coordination tasks (Two-Door, Navigation, and Giveway) demonstrate that these models provide interpretable causal representations under both full and partial observability, with model accuracy scaling directly with interventional strength.

</details>


### [92] [RAGuard: A Layered Defense Framework for Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems Against Data Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26339)
*Pushkal Kumar,Tucker Nielson,Tanish Kolhe,Shubham Zala,Vincent Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RAGuard分层防御体系，结合对抗性微调密集检索器与零知识推理补丁（ZKIP），在5-30%投毒率下将事实类语料库投毒攻击成功率降至0.000，同时保持Recall@5在清洁基线0.03范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖外部语料库，易受恶意注入段落操纵检索证据的语料库投毒攻击，现有防御缺乏无标签、黑盒且不依赖模型内部的通用方案。

Method: 分层防御：第一层在合成投毒文档（捏造事实、矛盾、推理陷阱）上对抗性微调密集检索器，使其降低恶意段落排序；第二层ZKIP为无标签黑盒过滤器，对每个检索文档执行留一解码，通过语义偏移与输出熵变化评分，无需投毒标签、真实答案及模型内部访问。

Result: 对抗性检索器单独使用虽降低攻击成功率但未消除；ZKIP在所有防御配置中将攻击成功率驱动至0.000，Recall@5保持在清洁基线0.03以内。监督分析证实ZKIP反事实信号蕴含可学习投毒结构。防御开销为k+1次生成器推理（k=5时6倍），提供批处理与早停近似降低开销。关键词保留投毒使BM25等词法检索器基本不受影响，界定威胁模型边界。

Conclusion: RAGuard通过分层设计实现高效、无标签、黑盒的事实类投毒防御，ZKIP作为核心组件显著消除攻击同时保持检索质量，代码与数据集已开源支持复现。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems ground large language models (LLMs) in external corpora, but this reliance exposes them to corpus poisoning: maliciously injected passages that manipulate retrieved evidence. We introduce RAGuard, a layered defense against \emph{factual} corpus-poisoning attacks on RAG pipelines. The first layer adversarially fine-tunes a dense retriever on synthetic poisoned documents (fabricated facts, contradictions, and reasoning traps), teaching it to downrank malicious passages before generation. The second layer, the Zero-Knowledge Inference Patch ZKIP, is a label-free, black-box filter: for each retrieved document, it performs a leave-one-out decode and scores the document by the semantic shift and output-entropy change that its removal induces. ZKIP requires no poison labels, no ground-truth answers, and no access to model internals; it compares the model's own answers under counterfactual contexts. On poisoned Natural Questions at 5--30\% poison ratios, adversarial retriever training alone reduces but does not eliminate attack success, while ZKIP drives the measured attack success rate to 0.000 in every defended configuration, keeping Recall@5 within 0.03 of the clean-corpus baseline. Supervised analyses on both Natural Questions and BEIR (NFCorpus) confirm that the counterfactual signals ZKIP relies on carry learnable poison structure. The defense costs $k{+}1$ generator passes per query ($6\times$ for $k{=}5$); we analyze batching and early-stopping approximations that reduce this overhead. We also show that keyword-preserving poisons leave lexical retrievers such as BM25 essentially unaffected, an observation that delineates the boundary of the threat model. Code, datasets, and evaluation harnesses are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [93] [Automorphism-Induced Non-Canonicity in Top-k Explanations of Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26344)
*Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Kaizhen Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出基于梯度的GNN解释器在处理具有对称性（自同构）的分子图时存在结构性障碍：由于消息传递的排列等变性，化学等价基团（如两个硝基）获得相同的归因分数，但标准的top-k报告任意选择其中一个，导致解释的任意性。作者提出无参数判据判断是否必须分裂轨道，并在Lean 4中形式化验证。实验显示Mutagenicity数据集中93.4%分子有非平凡自同构，24%的含两个可互换硝基的分子在稀疏预算下任意选择一个。报告轨道而非单一边可消除任意性，仅增加微小开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN解释方法忽略输入图的自同构对称性，导致对化学等价基团的解释任意且不一致，这在分子数据集中普遍存在（93.4%有非平凡自同构），且标准解释报告（top-k边）会随机打破对称性，掩盖模型真实的决策依据。

Method: 提出基于图结构的无参数判据，判断给定稀疏预算k下，所有分数最优的解释报告是否必须分裂自同构轨道。在Lean 4中机械化形式化验证该判据，无公理依赖。在21298个实例-预算决策上与模型等价性检查完全一致。实验在Mutagenicity数据集上评估，对比模型盲区制造的对称性，并测量报告轨道的开销。

Result: 判据与机械模型等价性检查在所有测试案例中完全一致，未发现例外。Mutagenicity数据集中93.4%分子存在非平凡自同构；在文献报告的稀疏预算下，含两个可互换硝基的分子中有24%（25个中的6个）任意选择其中一个硝基。模型盲区导致化学上可区分的原子被视为对称，且分辨率由模型读取的特征决定而非参数化方式。报告轨道而非单一边可消除任意性，仅需0.11ms和每图额外0.43条边。

Conclusion: GNN解释中的对称性打破是结构性障碍而非实现缺陷。任何单值、最小且尊重对称性的解释规则在自同构群不固定最小有效解释时不可能同时满足。提出的判据可从图结构alone判断是否必须分裂轨道，报告轨道是消除任意性的有效方案，开销极小。这揭示了分子图解释中普遍存在的对称性问题，需在解释方法中显式处理自同构。

Abstract: A gradient-based GNN explainer given a molecule with two chemically equivalent nitro groups assigns them attribution scores that are equal to the last bit. It cannot do otherwise: message passing is exactly permutation equivariant, so any automorphism of the input leaves every attribution invariant. Yet the standard report, the top-k edges, names one of the two, and which one is settled by the order of an array. We show this is a structural obstruction rather than an implementation slip. When no minimal valid explanation is fixed by the input's automorphism group, no rule can be single-valued, minimal and symmetry-respecting at once. For the exact-k reports used in practice we give a parameter-free criterion, mechanised in Lean 4 with no axiom dependencies, that decides from the graph alone whether every score-optimal report of that size must split an orbit. Across 21298 instance-budget decisions the criterion agrees with a mechanical model-equivalence check without exception, and no severing case we found admitted a neutral alternative. The obstruction is common. Nontrivial automorphisms occur in 93.4% of Mutagenicity, the dataset the seminal explainability papers use, so the measure-zero dismissal of symmetric inputs, sound on the continuous domains it was made for, collapses here. At the sparsity budget those papers report, 24.0% of molecules with two interchangeable nitro groups (6 of 25) surface exactly one of them, every one arbitrary under mechanical verification. A model's blindness also manufactures symmetry: every MUTAG molecule contains atoms chemistry separates and the network provably cannot, and a matched control shows the resolution is set by what the model reads rather than how it is parameterised. Reporting orbits removes the arbitrariness at 0.11 ms and 0.43 extra edges per graph.

</details>


### [94] [MetaKoopman: Bayesian Meta-Learning of Koopman Operators for Modeling Structured Dynamics under Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26345)
*Mahmoud Selim,Sriharsha Bhat,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaKoopman是一个贝叶斯元学习框架，通过线性潜在表示建模非线性动力学，利用矩阵正态-逆威沙特先验实现闭式贝叶斯更新和后验预测分布，捕捉认知与偶然不确定性，在自动驾驶卡车拖车系统和仿真任务中展现出优于现有方法的多步预测精度、不确定性校准和分布偏移鲁棒性，并在实地实验中验证了其在极限工况下的运动规划有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实系统中的分布偏移（如恶劣冬季路况）使得非线性动力学建模与预测面临挑战，需要一种能够快速适应新环境、同时量化认知与偶然不确定性的方法，以支持鲁棒决策。

Method: 提出MetaKoopman框架，基于Koopman算子将非线性动力学线性化至潜在空间，引入矩阵正态-逆威沙特（MNIW）先验，实现基于近期轨迹片段的闭式贝叶斯更新，并给出未来状态轨迹的闭式后验预测分布，同时捕捉认知不确定性（模型参数）与偶然不确定性（过程噪声）。

Result: 在全尺度自动驾驶卡车拖车系统的雪地、冰面、混合摩擦等恶劣冬季场景及多种分布偏移仿真任务中，MetaKoopman在多步预测精度、不确定性校准和分布偏移鲁棒性上持续优于基线方法；实地实验表明其在紧急避让和极限抓地力工况下的动态可行运动规划表现优异。

Conclusion: MetaKoopman通过贝叶斯元学习与Koopman算子的结合，实现了对非线性动力学的快速适应与不确定性量化，为分布偏移下的鲁棒决策提供了有效工具，并在实际自动驾驶系统中验证了其工程价值。

Abstract: Modeling and forecasting nonlinear dynamics under distribution shifts is essential for robust decision-making in real-world systems. In this work, we propose MetaKoopman, a Bayesian meta-learning framework for modeling nonlinear dynamics through linear latent representations. MetaKoopman learns a Matrix Normal-Inverse Wishart (MNIW) prior over the Koopman operator, enabling closed-form Bayesian updates conditioned on recent trajectory segments. Moreover, it provides a closed-form posterior predictive distribution over future state trajectories, capturing both epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty in the learned dynamics. We evaluate MetaKoopman on a full-scale autonomous truck and trailer system across a wide range of adverse winter scenarios, including snow, ice, and mixed-friction conditions, as well as in simulated control tasks with diverse distribution shifts. MetaKoopman consistently outperforms prior approaches in multi-step prediction accuracy, uncertainty calibration, and robustness to distributional shifts. Field experiments further demonstrate its effectiveness in dynamically feasible motion planning, particularly during evasive maneuvers and operation at the limits of traction. Project website: https://mahmoud-selim.github.io/MetaKoopman/

</details>


### [95] [High-Order Markov Blanket Discovery via a k-Order Relaxation of the Faithfulness Assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26357)
*Loong Kuan Lee,Ragavi Krishnamoorthy,Nico Piatkowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“k阶”忠实性假设的放松，能捕捉k+2个变量间的奇偶型关系，并设计了k阶马尔可夫毯（kOMB）算法，实验表明该方法在忠实性假设被真实或经验违反时仍能恢复目标变量的马尔可夫毯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有马尔可夫毯学习方法普遍依赖忠实性假设，但该假设会被高阶依赖（如XOR、奇偶关系）及有限样本下的经验违反所破坏，导致错误依赖或遗漏真实依赖，因此需要一种能容忍此类违反的新方法。

Method: 提出k阶忠实性放松假设，捕捉k+2变量间的奇偶型关系；基于此设计k阶马尔可夫毯（kOMB）算法，作为概念验证用于马尔可夫毯发现。

Result: 实验表明kOMB算法在忠实性假设被真实违反（如奇偶关系）和经验违反（有限样本导致的虚假依赖）两种情况下，均能有效恢复目标变量的马尔可夫毯。

Conclusion: k阶忠实性放松为处理高阶依赖和有限样本违反提供了理论框架，kOMB算法展示了其在马尔可夫毯发现中的实用性，为因果发现、特征选择等领域提供了更鲁棒的工具。

Abstract: The problem of learning the graphical Markov blanket (MB) of a variable from data has applications in many areas such as structure learning for Bayesian networks and Markov random fields, causal discovery, and feature selection. However, a common assumption most methods make is that the conditional independencies in the distribution imply the same separation in the graphical structure -- also known as the faithfulness assumption. Unfortunately, this assumption can be violated by higher-order dependencies such as XOR and parity-type relations, and -- on finite samples -- by empirical violations that, in extreme cases, even induce spurious dependencies absent from the true distribution. Therefore, in this paper we propose a "k-order" relaxation of the faithfulness assumption that captures parity type relationships between k+2 variables. We then propose a proof of concept algorithm called k-order Markov blanket (kOMB) that uses this relaxation for MB discovery. Finally, we empirically show how kOMB can recover the MB of a variable under both true and empirical violations of faithfulness. Code available at: https://github.com/lklee9/k-order-Markov-blanket

</details>


### [96] [Post-Training at the Edge of Detectability: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26358)
*Keegan Harris,Brian W. Lee,Ian Waudby-Smith,Philip Amortila,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出博弈论框架为KL正则化RL中的正则化系数提供原则性选择方法，实现奖励与保持权衡的最优平衡，并在Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.2-1B上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL正则化RL目标缺乏原则性的正则化系数设定方法，实际中依赖启发式或超参数搜索，导致训练开销大或奖励-保持权衡次优。

Method: 构建序列博弈：智能体最大化累积奖励，监控者检测策略偏离参考策略。均衡策略等价于最优正则化系数下的KL正则化RL，该系数最大化单位统计可区分度的奖励。利用凹凸分数规划将系数学习归约为标准KL正则化RL目标，便于集成微调流程。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.2-1B上实验表明，该方法在持续学习设置下实现具有竞争力的奖励-保持权衡，并展示如何用框架审计提供开源模型的API服务商。

Conclusion: 该方法为KL正则化系数提供了具有统计解释的原则性选择，提升微调效率并支持模型服务审计。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning is widely used in language model training to improve model performance on a target task while limiting drift from a reference policy. A standard way to balance this trade-off is via a KL-regularized RL objective, although this formulation does not by itself provide a principled way to set the regularization coefficient. In practice, the coefficient is typically chosen heuristically or via hyperparameter search, which can lead to unnecessary overhead in training cost or undesirable reward-retention trade-offs. We instead propose a game-theoretic framework that gives this trade-off an explicit statistical interpretation. Specifically, we study a sequential game in which an agent chooses a policy to maximize cumulative reward while a monitor observes policy outputs over time and tests for deviations from the reference policy. Although not originating from the same perspective, we show that the resulting equilibrium policy can nonetheless be expressed as the solution to a KL-regularized RL problem for an optimal regularization parameter that can be viewed as maximizing reward per unit of statistical distinguishability. Drawing on classical results from concave-convex fractional programming, we provide a principled method for learning this equilibrium coefficient via reduction to the KL-regularized RL objective, thus allowing for flexible integration into standard fine-tuning pipelines. In experiments with Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.2-1B, we demonstrate that our methods result in competitive reward-retention trade-offs in a continual learning setting, and illustrate how our framework may be used to audit API providers serving open-source models.

</details>


### [97] [ClockRoPE: Random Fourier Rotations for Temporal Routine Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26369)
*Yiwen Chen,Joshua Ainslie,Krzysztof Choromanski,Xiang Gao,Su-Lin Wu,Yiping Yuan,Qian Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ClockRoPE，一种基于随机傅里叶旋转的位置编码方法，用于序列推荐中的周期性建模，解决了 RoPE 在复杂距离相关模式下的局限，并在线上 A/B 测试中显著提升核心指标，已部署于大规模生成式检索系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE 的对数线性频率调度虽能产生长程注意力衰减，但难以捕捉序列推荐等领域中的复杂距离相关模式（如时间周期性），限制了其在该类任务中的应用。

Method: 研究表明任意归一化连续正定注意力调制函数均可由其傅里叶变换诱导的随机旋转逼近，称为随机傅里叶旋转。基于此提出 ClockRoPE，从周期性注意力调制函数导出旋转频率，实现常规建模。

Result: 在线上 A/B 测试中，ClockRoPE 在受重视的用户参与度指标上持续优于基线，并已成功部署于主流视频分享平台的生产级生成式检索系统。

Conclusion: ClockRoPE 通过引入随机傅里叶旋转理论，有效建模序列数据中的周期性模式，解决了 RoPE 在复杂距离相关性下的表达不足，具有显著的工程落地价值。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) has been widely adopted in transformer-based large language models. However, its log-linear frequency schedule, originally designed to produce long-term attention decay, limits its adoption in domains with more complex distance-correlation patterns, such as temporal periodicity in sequential recommendation. We investigate the expressiveness of general query/key rotations and find that any normalized continuous positive-definite attention modulation function can be approximated by random rotations induced by its own Fourier transform, which we term Random Fourier Rotations. Building on this theory, we propose ClockRoPE for routine modeling in sequential recommendation, where rotation frequencies are derived from periodic attention modulation functions. In online A/B tests, ClockRoPE demonstrates consistent improvements in valued engagement metrics, and has been successfully deployed in production-scale generative retrieval system at a major video-sharing platform.

</details>


### [98] [Q-Steer: Action-Value Guidance for Molecular Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26391)
*Xinyu Wang,Jinbo Bi,Minghu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-Steer是一种卷积时动作价值引导原语，通过离线训练的前缀动作价值评分器（PAVS-Q）估计部分SMILES前缀下候选下一令牌的下游奖励，并将归一化价值奖励添加到采样logits中，在不改变在线Oracle预算的情况下提升分子优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Oracle受限分子优化中的延迟反馈问题：奖励仅在完整分子生成后给出，导致策略优化近视，优化器只知分子好坏却不知哪些中间动作导致好坏。

Method: 提出Q-Steer，使用离线训练并冻结的前缀动作价值评分器（PAVS-Q），估计在部分SMILES前缀下采取候选下一令牌的下游奖励，将归一化价值奖励加到采样logits上。优化器更新规则和在线Oracle预算保持不变。

Result: 在PMO23基准上，固定10,000次在线调用预算，跨两个分子语言模型骨干和四个优化器的完全因子实验显示，Q-Steer在所有八个骨干-优化器组合中均提升平均有效唯一分数，宏平均分数增益+0.033至+0.049，每组合获得18-20个任务胜利。机制控制显示动作身份关键：前缀广播价值近乎中性，打乱动作价值损害性能。

Conclusion: Q-Steer作为一种可复用的卷积时动作价值包装器，无需改变在线Oracle预算，即可跨优化器家族和策略骨干提升平均分子优化奖励。

Abstract: Oracle-limited molecular optimization gives reward only after a complete molecule is generated, while each rollout requires many local next-token decisions. This delayed-feedback interface makes molecular policy optimization myopic: an optimizer can learn that a molecule was good without knowing which intermediate actions made it good. We introduce Q-Steer, a rollout-time action-value steering primitive for molecular language models. Q-Steer uses an offline-trained and frozen prefix-action value scorer, PAVS-Q, that estimates the downstream reward of taking a candidate next token under a partial SMILES prefix, then adds a normalized value bonus to sampling logits. The optimizer update rule and online oracle budget are unchanged; the claim is fixed-online-oracle performance, not equal total compute. On PMO23 with a fixed 10,000-call online budget, complete factorial studies across two molecular language-model backbones and four optimizers show that Q-Steer improves mean valid-unique score in all eight backbone-optimizer cells, with positive macro mean-score gains between +0.033 and +0.049 and 18-20 task wins per cell. Mechanism controls show that action identity matters: prefix-broadcast values are nearly neutral, while shuffled action values harm performance. These results support Q-Steer as a reusable rollout-time action-value wrapper that improves average molecular optimization reward across optimizer families and policy backbones without changing the online oracle budget.

</details>


### [99] [Flow Map Learning via Nongradient Vector Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26398)
*Mark Goldstein,Anshuk Uppal,Raghav Singhal,Aahlad Puli,Rajesh Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SGFlow 是一种学习流映射的方法，避免了显式可逆性约束和昂贵的模型迭代微分，通过非守恒动力学训练模型计算 ODE 解和隐含速度，在 CIFAR 基准上表现优异且具有理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散和流模型推理开销大，一致性模型虽直接学习流映射但面临计算挑战（需模型逆或反向传播迭代），且缺乏理论证明。SGFlow 旨在解决这些问题。

Method: SGFlow 通过非守恒动力学训练模型，使其从头计算 ODE 解和隐含速度，具有驻点保证，避免显式可逆约束和昂贵的模型迭代微分。

Result: 在 CIFAR 图像基准上，SGFlow 在 10 采样步数下达到最佳 FID，在其他步数下与流匹配、Meanflow、拉格朗日映射匹配竞争，且是唯一具有驻点理论保证的 stopgrad 动力学方法。

Conclusion: SGFlow 提供了一种高效且理论可靠的学习流映射方法，在保持竞争性能的同时解决了现有方法的计算难题。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based models benefit from simple regression losses, but inference incurs significant overhead because sampling requires integration. Consistency models address this by directly learning the flow maps along the ODE trajectory, opening a design space between one-step and many-step approaches. However, existing methods face computational challenges such as requiring model inverses or backpropagation through iterated model calls, and do not always prove that the desired ODE flow map is a solution to the loss.
